I keep thinking I’m messing everything up… like I don’t really belong in the apprenticeship, and I’m letting people down over things I know other people would probably just handle.\n\nDoctor: Do you often worry about the future?\n\nPatient: I mean… a little, I guess. Other people would probably just fix it and move on, but I can’t seem to.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad or empty most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… I wouldn’t say most of the time. I’ve felt pretty down on myself, I guess, but “sad or empty” is harder to say.\n\nDoctor: Have you been enjoying things you usually like to do?\n\nPatient: Not really. I mean… I used to draw a lot, play volleyball, keep up with this volunteer group. Lately I just kind of walk past my sketchbook or ignore the texts. It doesn’t really feel like much.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel guilty about things you've done or not done?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I do. Mostly about work and the apprenticeship. I keep feeling like I’ve ruined things for everyone, or like I’m kind of a fake there. Even small mistakes feel like I’m letting my family down, and the younger apprentices too… even if other people would probably just fix it and move on.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel nervous or on edge?\n\nPatient: I guess sometimes, yeah. Not all the time. I haven’t really been keeping track of it.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost a significant amount of weight recently?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I haven’t really been checking, but my clothes seem about the same.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel like you're not good enough?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I do. That’s kind of why I came in. I keep feeling like I’ve ruined things for everybody, like I’m just faking my way through the apprenticeship. Even stuff other people would probably just fix and move on from, I take it like I let my family down… and the younger apprentices too.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're not good enough most of the time?\n\nPatient: Most of the time lately, yeah. I keep thinking I’m messing everything up… like I don’t really belong in the apprenticeship, and I’m letting people down over things I know other people would probably just handle.\n\nDoctor: Do you often worry about the future?\n\nPatient: I mean… a little, I guess. I try not to think too far ahead. It just kind of feels uncertain.\n\nDoctor: Do you find pleasure in the things you're doing nowadays?\n\nPatient: Not really… not like I used to. I’ll walk right past my sketchbook, skip volleyball, ignore the volunteer group texts… and I don’t even feel much about it. Those used to be the things that felt like me.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel worthless?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I do. I keep feeling like I’ve ruined things for everybody, like I’m just faking my way through the apprenticeship. Like I let my family down, and the younger apprentices too, over things other people would probably just fix and move on from.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Um… probably around nine months. It’s been there a while, but it got harder to keep it together once school and work started slipping.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the last nine months where you felt better for at least a couple of months?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. It’s kind of been this same stretch for about nine months… maybe a little up and down, but I don’t think I had a couple months where I actually felt better. It just got harder to hide once school and work started slipping.\n\nDoctor: Are you usually feeling this way for most of the day?\n\nPatient: Yeah… most days, it’s there a lot. I can be doing other things, but it keeps coming back — like I messed everything up, or I’m just not really good enough to be there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt this way before, like you're not good enough and messing everything up, in a period of time before these past nine months?\n\nPatient: Not really like this before. I mean, I’ve felt unsure of myself, but this… feeling like I ruined things for everyone, like I’m some kind of fake in the apprenticeship, and that I let my family and the younger apprentices down… that’s more what’s been happening lately.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period in your life where you felt similarly down or not good enough before these past nine months?\n\nPatient: No… not really before this. I’ve felt bad about messing up before, but this is different. Lately it’s more like I ruined things for everybody, like I’m a fake in the apprenticeship and I let people down over stuff other people would probably just fix and move on from.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had another period like this in your life before the past nine months?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think so. Not like this. This feeling that I ruined things and let everyone down — that’s what made me come in.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had an episode like this before in your life that lasted for a while and then got better on its own?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. Not one that lasted like this and then just went away on its own.\n\nDoctor: Have you had more than one period like this in your life?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think so. Just this one, as far as I can tell.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any episodes like this before in your life that got better on their own?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think so. Not that I can remember, anyway.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out.
